# Old Agreements pointers, part 1 of 3: the core

17 November 2026. Written by Claude, the scribe's session P1. A list only: no text in the repository is changed by it, and nothing in it is a decision, a proposal of wording, or a fix.

**The wish** (Nobody, allegedly, quoted from the ledger, `docs/decisions.md`, branch `claude/lead-records-2`): "I've decided for a total rewrite of agreements from scratch." "No translation, fresh from the research we had all agreed was excellent direction." And what must hold: "can I with a single click reward all the people that contributed to the service or good via MOR". On now: "Before redrafting any layer I want to really look at the findings in details, find the right questions and then find one last way for me to zoom out before deciding on the rewrite."

**Why** (the MOR lead's wording): to show him how big the rewrite really is, before he decides it.

**What was read.** Draft 25 (`docs/agreements-from-scratch/draft-25.md`, branch `claude/agreements-clean-1-hahuwn`, pull request 183): sections 1 to 5 and 8.1, the rest where a place needed it; on the same branch `encoding-2.md`, sections 2 to 6, and `docs/lead/proposals/report-block-4.md`. The old Agreements (`spec/MIR-agreements-draft-24.md`, main) as a lookup only: its "Words used in this MIR", and rules by number. This part's files, on main, latest versions: `spec/02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, `spec/MIR-identity-draft-16.md`, `spec/MIR-text-draft-11.md`, `spec/MIR-envelopes-draft-12.md`, `spec/MIR-money-draft-14.md`, `spec/MIR-development-draft-12.md`, `spec/README.md`.

## In plain words

1. In the seven core files (the MICS, which is the core spec over all layers, five layer specs and the README of `spec/`), 1,034 places lean on the old Agreements; more than half of them (536) are in the MICS, the core document itself.
2. 557 places ("a concept gone") rest on something draft 25 has no counterpart for at all: collectives, keepers, grants and scoped keys, publications, stakes, splits, claims, termination.
3. 305 places ("a rule to rewrite") state a rule draft 25 reads another way (two rival acts both stop, nothing an agent made is set aside, nothing ever ends); 172 ("a word to change") need only a word or a number changed.
4. Of all those places, 202 are also marked "unclear": draft 25 does not say what stands there, most often because it leaves the other layers' side open (the payment rules an agreement names (a cMIP), `objects` and `acks`, whether its own spec hash is "the Agreements MIR", what a vow is as an act).
5. Three layers below have whole mechanisms built for the old Agreements: Identity's keyless genesis for a collective and its scoped keys, Money's payment task and receipts that name an ownership agreement, Development's task numbers 8 to 15.

## How to read this list

- **Who wrote it.** P1 is this session: the first of three that each list one part of the repository, which a fourth, P4, joins. "The ledger" is `docs/decisions.md`, where what Nobody, allegedly, decided is recorded.
- **The seven core files** are the seven listed under "What was read": the MICS (`spec/02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, the MOR Inscribed Core Spec, the document over all six layers), the five layer specs other than Agreements (Identity, Text, Envelopes, Money, Development) and the README of `spec/`.
- **The three kinds**, in the last column, are exactly: **a word to change** (the idea survives in draft 25 under another name or number); **a rule to rewrite** (the sentence states behaviour that draft 25 reads another way); **a concept gone** (what it rests on has no counterpart in draft 25). Every row has one kind. "Unclear" is not a kind: it is written in the draft-25 column, beside a kind, where what draft 25 has there cannot be told. So the 202 unclear rows are counted inside the three kinds as well.
- **"Nothing: dropped"** covers every item cited in "what it leans on" unless the cell names them one by one.
- **Abbreviations.** d24: the old Agreements, `spec/MIR-agreements-draft-24.md`. d25: draft 25. W01 to W57: entries of the working list below. G1 to G14: draft 25's grammar rules (G4.8 is clause 8 of G4). K1 to K8: draft 25's tasks, each a "socket": a field of an act where a cMIP plugs in. P1 to P21: draft 25's open parameters (its section 6). E1 to E22: rules of `encoding-2.md`, draft 25's acts as bytes. F followed by a number (F282): a finding of `docs/findings/`, as the quoted file cites it.
- **Words of the protocol used here.** A **cMIP** is a document, named by its hash, that a field of an act names to answer one question (draft 25, section 2); anyone may write one. A **fork** is two acts that each claim to follow the same act; each of the two is a **tip** (G12.1). A **verifier** is the software that applies the rules to acts and gives answers.
- **The section column** gives the heading the sentence sits under; a word in brackets after it, as "(clone)", names the paragraph within that section.
- **Quotes** are copied exactly; "…" marks a cut. A quote in the draft-25 column is draft 25's own words.


## The working list: concepts of draft 24 that draft 25 dropped or renamed

Built from draft 24's "Words used in this MIR" against draft 25's section 2 (with sections 3 and 5 and encoding-2 for act types and fields). A working list, not a decision. "d24" is spec/MIR-agreements-draft-24.md; "d25" is draft-25.md.

### Act types and the act's shape

| W | Draft 24 | Draft 25 | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| W01 | Agreements act types: 0 Terms, 1 Signature, 2 Keeper record, 3 Claim, 4 Stake transfer, 5 Release, 6 Publication, 8 Split plan and split, 9 Grant, 10 Revocation, 12 Liveness, 13 Abandonment declaration, 14 Contest, 16 Resignation, 17 Record, 18 Negotiation message, 19 Fork, 20 Closing, 22 Settlement request, 23 Fork settlement, 24 Notice, 25 Request to a judge | two act types: 0 Terms, 1 Statement (encoding-2, E2); a report is type 0 under the report block's own spec hash, a proposal | every type but 0 and 1 dropped; type 1 renamed and widened |
| W02 | Terms (type 0): parties, text, cMIPs, keepers, rules; terms fields numbered in d24 (field 2 tasks, 4 mark, 7 stake, 15 extensions, 18 constitutional rule, 19 layer rules, 21 chain of judgment, 22 departed members, 23, 33 target, 35 control model, 36 termination terms, 37 termination mark, ...) | Terms (type 0) with fields 0 to 19 of encoding-2 section 4 (`replaces`, `agreement`, `parties`, `authors`, `body`, `relies_on`, `in_force`, `powers`, `place`, `clock`, `receiver`, `envelope`, `envelope_at`, `condition`, `control`, `calls`, `data`, `resolves`, `continues`, `previous`) | same word, new fields; every d24 field number is gone |
| W03 | Signature (type 1): one party naming terms (or a clone, publication, transfer, release) as agreed | Statement (type 1) of kind `assent`; a Statement may also be a counter signature, contest, resolve or a K7 kind. "Signature of an agreement" in d25 means something else: an act made in an agreement's name under a power (G1.2) | renamed |
| W04 | `objects` (and `acks`) on an act name the agreement chain and the act it follows ("belongs to") | an Agreements act MUST NOT carry `objects` or `acks` (encoding-2, E3); what an act names is in its payload (`agreement`, `terms`, `replaces`, `previous`, `relies_on`...) | dropped for Agreements acts |
| W05 | Agreement id = id of its terms act | the root: the fingerprint (act id) of the first Terms act | kept, renamed "root" |
| W06 | Agreement chain: every act on one agreement (signatures, clones, records, transfers, contests) | "chain" is only the versions of one agreement, by `replaces`; one signer's acts within an agreement are a "line", by `previous` (G6, G12.1) | split and renamed |
| W07 | Clone, never modify (rule 45); a clone; complete clone | update: a new version naming the one before in `replaces` (G4.8); "the original" is the version an update would replace | renamed |
| W08 | Mark (field 4): which change rule a clone claims to meet; false mark (rule 46) | nothing: the update condition answers valid or not valid (G4.11) | dropped |
| W09 | Change rules: constitutional change rule (field 18), rule of each layer (field 19), control-model powers; "every party" in a deal (rule 47) | the update condition (`condition`, task K4), default "everyone who signed the original" (G4.11(e)); "constitution" is d25's word for the update condition (G5.6) | renamed |
| W10 | Power: right to bring a change into force | power: an entry in `powers` saying a Statement of a kind, or a resolving update, binds when a rule says yes; no power makes Terms replace Terms (G5.2) | same word, meaning changed |
| W11 | Termination, active/terminated, termination terms (task 15, field 36), termination mark (field 37), a deal's end | "never ended": an agreement only gets a new version; a verifier MUST NOT answer that Terms or an agreement have ended (G4.9) | dropped |

## The places marked "unclear"

Every row whose draft-25 column says "unclear", with its one line of why. First the ones on which a verifier's answer would turn (the readers' and the scribe's judgment of which those are, not a decision), then the rest in file order.

### Where a verifier's answer would turn on it

- **C-037** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, MIR: Identity ("What a rotation keeps")): "Acts outside it, or disowned inside it, are void, unless another identity acknowledged them (by an Identity, Money or Agreements act) or…" — Agreements acts MUST NOT carry `acks` (encoding-2, E3); keeper: nothing, "place" stands in (G7); unclear whether d25's naming in payload (`relies_on`, `answers`) counts as acknowledging
- **C-115** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Agreements and deals): "Named by its line, it carries what it signed through every later version; loops are refused, a verifier that cannot follow every layer a…" — named by its root (G1.1); signing is "one level" (G1.2); receivers followed until a party is reached (G1.4); unclear: d25 says nothing of loops refused or nesting bounded at eight
- **C-144** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Agreements and deals (two versions)): "**A settlement is final for everything its signers could see**, which, with no clock, is what its history holds: a version on a dropped…" — resolving update (G12.3); late update replaces nothing where knots or an anchoring show it (G4.7); rival resolving updates (G12.7); unclear whether d25 reads a tip the resolving update did not name as "nothing reopened"
- **C-130** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Agreements and deals (clone)): "A clone never reduces a share of a stake without its holder's signature, member or not." — unclear: shares are read from the Terms' words (K1); which parts an update may change, and who must sign, is the update condition's (G4.11, section 5.2); default everyone who signed the original (G4.11(e))
- **C-214** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Publications, splits and time (shares by reference)): "**The payment decides**: a receipt naming another tag or version than the payment's commitment is a wrong receipt, shown as such, counti…" — unclear: d25 shows two reports naming the same settlement as matched or mismatched (G11.4), and chooses neither; "counting for nothing" is not said of a receipt
- **C-012** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Structure ("From bake to publication")): "*Unless there is a publication, money sent to a bake goes to its uploader, with no split possible.*" — unclear: d25 reads the baker (holder of the baker's rights at the act cited) from the envelope's chain (G14); where money sent to a bake goes is not said in d25 (Money's, section 8.1)
- **C-193** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Publications, splits and time (price)): "Unless there is a publication, the tip goes to the uploader, with no split possible.*" — unclear: d25 reads the holder of the baker's rights from the envelope's chain (G14.1); where a tip goes is not said
- **C-686** (`MIR-envelopes-draft-12.md`, Rule 23. Money sent to a bare bake is its uploader's): "*Unless there is a publication, the tip goes to the uploader, with no split possible.*" — offer (section 2); dividing a cMIP's (K6); unclear: G14 gives the baker's rights to the holder at a transfer, which this sentence does not see
- **C-073** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, MIR: Money ("The payee chooses its rails")): "A receipt or claim counts only on one of those, and, under an agreement, only on one implementing that agreement's payment cMIP." — unclear: d25 Terms name no payment cMIP; a payment names the version it read (G13.2)
- **C-731** (`MIR-money-draft-14.md`, Rule 4. The payee decides which rails it accepts): "*In short: the agreement names the payment cMIP; the payee's pointer and vault name the rails; a receipt on any other rail counts for no…" — unclear: d25's Terms have no payment socket (K1..K8); a payment holds K6 (G11.7)
- **C-734** (`MIR-money-draft-14.md`, Rule 4. The payee decides which rails it accepts): "where the payment falls under an agreement, that rail Module implements the payment cMIP the agreement names (Development, field 5)." — unclear: d25's Terms name no payment cMIP (K1..K8); G11.7
- **C-843** (`MIR-money-draft-14.md`, Part four, Tasks): "**Payment.** One payment cMIP per agreement or publication fills this task." — unclear: d25's Terms have no payment socket; a payment holds K6 (G11.7)
- **C-730** (`MIR-money-draft-14.md`, Words: Routes, First payer): "The payer of a route's first hop; the buyer of a purchase the route pays, where their claim says so (rule 24)." — buyer is a signer of the offer's Terms by assent (section 2 "Buyer", G11.7); unclear: d25 does not read a buyer from a payment claim
- **C-807** (`MIR-money-draft-14.md`, Rule 24. A valid route): "The first payer of a route is the payer of its first hop (its receipt's field 2); the first payer's claim of that hop names in key 4 wha…" — unclear: d25 reads a buyer from an assent, not from a citation in a claim (G11.7)
- **C-760** (`MIR-money-draft-14.md`, Rule 15. Discharge, and an obligation's states): "closed by clone;" — update (G4.8); unclear: d25 does not say an update closes anything owed under the version it replaces (G13.1)
- **C-640** (`MIR-text-draft-11.md`, Rule 14. What a text act carries): "Any other `objects` on a text act, whoever signs it and with whatever key, is invalid." — unclear: draft 25 says nothing of text acts; if the two cases go, what "any other" excepts goes with them
- **C-643** (`MIR-text-draft-11.md`, Rule 14, the Text-only verifier): "It answers unknown for a text act carrying exactly one object of either allowed shape, and invalid for any other `objects`." — unclear: draft 25 gives no shape a text act could carry; it does not speak of text acts
- **C-650** (`MIR-text-draft-11.md`, Rule 6. What you sign is what you saw): "Before signing terms, a grant or a clone (Agreements), a client MUST show every bidirectional control visibly, as an escape, in that pla…" — Terms (type 0, E2) covers all three: a delegation is Terms (G5.5), an update is Terms with `replaces` (G4.8); unclear: whether an assent or another Statement (type 1, whose `content` may hold text, section 3.2) is meant; draft 25 writes its one client duty in G12.8 "and nowhere else"
- **C-677** (`MIR-envelopes-draft-12.md`, Rule 19. An act that may not acknowledge, and does, is invalid): "*A verifier needs the six MIR hashes to apply this, as it needs them for everything else; one that cannot tell which MIR an act's specif…" — Agreements acts are written under `SPEC_DRAFT_25`, the hash of a file in `docs/` (E2); unclear whether that hash is "the Agreements MIR's" for this rule
- **C-979** (`MIR-development-draft-12.md`, Freeze scenarios, Part two): "The six MIR hashes stable wherever uploaded; a read-only client recomputes them from the texts it ships, and shows them; two clients bui…" — unclear: d25's hash `SPEC_DRAFT_25` is computed the same way (E2) but whether it is one of the six is not said
- **C-461** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, MIR: Development ("Adoption by opt-in")): "Below the MICS the one trust root is the six frozen MIR texts, uploaded, and committed in the genesis repository, as canonical text (…);…" — acts of Agreements are written under d25's own spec hash, `SPEC_DRAFT_25`, of a file in `docs/` on its branch, computed as the core computes a MIR's (encoding-2, E2; d25 preamble); unclear whether it is one of the six frozen texts
- **C-693** (`MIR-envelopes-draft-12.md`, 9. Vows): "A vow is an act of type 5 of this MIR, naming in words **something MOR cannot hash, existing or to come**: a good in stock or to be made…" — "vow": "the same thing" as an offer "but without bytes attached" (section 2); unclear whether Envelopes type 5 stays, and what "bytes attached" means (8.4)
- **C-060** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, MIR: Envelopes ("Vows")): "**Vows.** A vow names in words something MOR cannot hash, existing or to come: its first act is its genesis, whose id is its name for go…" — "vow": "the same thing" as an offer "but without bytes attached" (section 2); what "bytes attached" means is open (section 8.4); unclear whether Envelopes keeps a vow act
- **C-1005** (`README.md`, Earlier drafts, table): "Envelopes draft 8, a transition draft, … draft 7 written for plain reading in Agreements draft 11's and Money draft 7's style, …, "vow"…" — vow: "the same thing" as an offer "but without bytes attached", no states (section 2, G11.8); unclear: whether Envelopes type 5 is still the act draft 25's "vow" means
- **C-552** (`MIR-identity-draft-16.md`, Rule 1. Genesis): "An identity whose genesis declares no signing key has none for good: its rotations install none (rotation check 4), and it signs no ever…" — nothing: dropped (W39); unclear: whether the keyless genesis form has any use left without collectives
- **C-544** (`MIR-identity-draft-16.md`, Words: Scoped key): "The act that installed it is judged by the next rotation like any act of the key it replaces (rules 33 to 35), so a scoped key installed…" — unclear: no d25 act installs a key; what a change of key does to a delegation's Terms or a line is listed as needed from the Sign layer (section 8.1)
- **C-557** (`MIR-identity-draft-16.md`, Rule 5. The verifier runs the checks): "In particular, it MUST check that the signature is valid for the key it names, and that the binding names an act in the signer's identit…" — nothing for the scoped-key branch: dropped (W50); unclear: whether another MIR installs scoped keys
- **C-945** (`MIR-development-draft-12.md`, Part three: format (CDDL)): "? 2 => [+ task-ref],        ; tasks it fills (required for a cMIP filling a task)" — unclear: d25's tasks K1..K8 carry no number a `task-ref` could hold (section 5)
- **C-948** (`MIR-development-draft-12.md`, Part three: format (CDDL)): "task-ref = [ mir: hash, task: uint ]" — unclear: d25's tasks have no number, and its hash is `SPEC_DRAFT_25` of a docs/ file (E2)
- **C-950** (`MIR-development-draft-12.md`, How the content is decoded, step 7): "Fields 2, 3, 4, 6, 7 and 8 may sit on any kind; fields 3 and 4 serve discovery only, and field 2 is read where an agreement names the cM…" — a socket names a cMIP by spec hash (E7); unclear: d25 does not read a cMIP's field 2
- **C-896** (`MIR-development-draft-12.md`, Rule 9. Modules within cMIPs): "Several Modules may operate at once under the one cMIP an agreement names for a task (for example several rail Modules under the payment…" — "Modules where needed": a module is named by the cMIP in the socket (section 5; 5.3); unclear: d25 has the cMIP name the module, Development has the Module name the cMIP in field 5

### The rest, in file order

- **C-003** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Purpose): "It describes identities, media, money, agreements and specifications, never what anyone does with them." — unclear: d25 still describes agreements, but does not say whether it stays a MIR in `spec/` (W54, W55)
- **C-004** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Structure): "They are numbered in this order wherever a format names a layer: 0 Identity, 1 Text, 2 Envelopes, 3 Money, 4 Agreements, 5 Development." — unclear: d25 does not number layers; it names operations State, Sign, Deliver, Order, Control (W54)
- **C-005** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Structure (table, row 2 Envelopes)): "Acts (outside and locked inside), public and private, key delivery, chains, media and the bake, vows, relays, delivery." — unclear: d25's "vow" is "the same thing" as an offer "but without bytes attached", no states (section 2, G11.8); d25 does not say whether Envelopes keeps a vow act
- **C-006** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Structure (table, row 3 Money)): "Obligations, settlement receipts and payer claims across rails, payee pointers, flow and vault, the price tag" — unclear: d25 has no price tag; a payment names the version it read (G13.2), how is Money's (section 8.1); receiver (G1.3) in place of where money ends; "settlement" is Money's evidence (G8.5); ways of paying left to Money (P20)
- **C-011** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Structure ("From bake to publication")): "Ownership is one thing, distribution another: one work has one ownership agreement and may have many publications, each with its own pri…" — unclear: d25 has works and offers as plain words and a receiver that may be an agreement (G1.3, G11.1 sequence), shares read by the body cMIP (K1), dividing a cMIP's (K6); it does not say one work has one ownership agreement
- **C-015** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Structure (collective)): "*Stated cost: a client knowing only Identity, or only Money, judges nothing a collective does; it shows every act for a collective unkno…" — nothing: dropped (W39); unclear whether a stated cost applies to paying an agreement as receiver (G1.3, G1.4)
- **C-016** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Structure (collective)): "Customers use agreements; only tips do not.*" — the buyer's leg: a buyer assents to the offer's Terms and signs a payment naming the version it read (G11.7, G13.2); tips: unclear, d25 does not say
- **C-018** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Structure ("Tasks")): "Tasks exist only where the MICS defines them; the full list is in Development." — d25's own tasks K1..K8 are listed in d25 section 5; unclear whether Development's list holds them
- **C-019** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Structure ("Tasks")): "A hand-off with no task number is no task: a proven offer names its own proof checker." — d25: every field of an act that names a cMIP is a task, and nothing else is (section 2); proven delivery: nothing as a rule, "delivery" is a module (section 5.3); unclear whether "proven offer" is d24's
- **C-023** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Structure ("Client conformance")): "A client must implement every MIR whose acts it acts on: a wallet that only sends tips needs Money; a client that acts on agreements nee…" — unclear: d25 does not say whether it stays a MIR in `spec/` (W54, W55)
- **C-029** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Common conventions ("The verdict vocabulary")): "*Counts for nothing* is an umbrella over void, not done, uncited, outside an area and in a broken stretch, as the Agreements MIR uses th…" — d25 uses "counts for nothing" of an invalid act and of acts not shown (section 2, G14.3); done, area, broken stretch: nothing; "uncited": unclear
- **C-030** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Common conventions ("The verdict vocabulary")): "The records a client shows (*disputed, contested, creator mismatch, wrong receipt, bypassing, mismatch, not found, deferred*) are displa…" — d25: "disputed" is a qualifier for acts resting on a branch set aside (G12.5); contest is a Statement (G11.8); creator mismatch: nothing (claims dropped); matched, mismatched, one side only, unanswered, no report called for are the report block's (G11.4); unclear which of the others are d24's
- **C-038** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, MIR: Identity ("What a rotation keeps")): "Evidence another identity signed (a receipt, an objection, an absence statement, a cosignature, an acknowledgement) counts only while it…" — d25 "disputed" is a qualifier on answers resting on a branch set aside (G12.5), and d25 says the core's meaning differs (section 2); unclear what "its pair" names in d25
- **C-039** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, MIR: Identity ("What a rotation keeps")): "An act bound to a rotation an escape abandons is judged the same way (evidence so bound, as when voided by its signer's rotation, decide…" — keeper: nothing (G7 place); rule 10: nothing in Agreements, the baker's rights pass by a transfer in the envelope's chain (G14); unclear what "complete" maps to

- **C-090** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, MIR: Money ("Only identities have payee pointers")): "A payment goes to the pointer of the payee its price tag names, or, sent to a bare bake, its uploader's." — unclear: d25 has no price tag; a payment names the version it read (G13.2), receiver for an act (G1.4)

- **C-093** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, MIR: Agreements (heading)): "## MIR: Agreements" — unclear: draft 25 is a file in `docs/` with its own spec hash (encoding-2, E2); it does not say whether it becomes the Agreements MIR in `spec/`
- **C-095** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, MIR: Agreements (intro)): "Money moves a payment to a payee pointer; Agreements say how it is shared, what is owed when something is not done, and how a group of p…" — d25: receiver (G1.3, G1.4); dividing is a cMIP's (K6, with a rule as module); "the grammar has no separate notion of a collective" (section 2, "Shares"); what is owed: unclear, only "what is still owed" in a leg report (K6)

- **C-107** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Agreements and deals): "**Everyone signs the founding terms.**" — unclear: d25 has no founding terms; the default of G4.1 asks an assent of every party named as a signer, an `in_force` rule may ask otherwise
- **C-117** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Agreements and deals): "**A deal's version, once relied on, stays complete.**" — unclear: d25 has no "complete"; Terms are in force by K2/G4.1; what others relied on stands applies to acts resting on a branch set aside (G12.5)

- **C-122** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Agreements and deals): "**What you sign is what you saw.**" — unclear: d25 writes one client duty only (G12.8); "nothing is shown to anyone before they sign" is said by a body cMIP (K1) and the client (section 5.2)
- **C-123** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Agreements and deals): "Before signing, clients show the plain text, with bidirectional controls visible (invisible characters that can reorder how text display…" — unclear: d25 keeps one client duty, "written here and nowhere else" (G12.8); a body cMIP (K1) and the client (section 5.2)
- **C-132** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Agreements and deals (clone)): "**No advance permission reaches what one holder is paid unlike the others**: a rule of the collective's control model the holder accepte…" — nothing: dropped; unclear whether any of it is an update condition's (G4.11)
- **C-145** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Agreements and deals (two versions)): "*Stated cost: whoever signed a version the settlement's signers could not see loses it; nobody who settled in good faith loses anything.*" — unclear: d25 G12.3, G12.7 and G4.7 decide; no such stated cost in d25 known to this reader
- **C-146** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Agreements and deals (two versions)): "A settlement that holds a newer version of a branch than the one it names settles nothing; one naming a version the verifier does not ho…" — resolving update names every tip (G12.3); unclear how d25 reads one naming a tip not held (G9 unchecked)
- **C-161** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Claims and ownership): "It carries no split, no fees and no split service: **ownership is one thing, distribution another.**" — unclear: a work in d25 is any agreement; its Terms may name a `receiver` that is an agreement (G1.3) and a `control` cMIP (K6); d25 does not keep them apart
- **C-166** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Claims and ownership (stake)): "Any act that changes a share needs its holder's signature." — unclear: who must sign an update that rewrites a share is the update condition's (G4.11, section 5.2), default everyone who signed the original (G4.11(e))
- **C-174** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Claims and ownership (vow)): "*Somebody owns the vow." — unclear: d25 gives the vow no meaning beyond the word (section 2)

- **C-188** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Publications, splits and time): "**One work, one ownership agreement, many publications**, each with its own price, partners and split, the owners' shares read by refere…" — unclear: d25 has works and offers as plain words; an offer may name a work as `receiver` by its root (G1.3), a sequence (G11.1); "one ownership agreement" per work is not said
- **C-191** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Publications, splits and time (price)): "A payment naming the publication itself, with no tag, is a tip through it: divided as a sale is, buying nothing." — unclear: a payment names the version it read (G13.2) and is a leg to the agreement's receiver (G11.1); a tip with no assent is not said in d25
- **C-192** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Publications, splits and time (price)): "*A tip to a work goes through the publication where it was found, which pays its partners and gives the owners their line." — unclear: receiver for an act followed through a sequence (G1.4, G11.1); partners and owners' line: nothing

- **C-203** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Publications, splits and time (deal complete)): "Otherwise a refund is owed, to the payer the payment committed to, an anonymous payer's fresh identity included (Money)." — unclear: d25 says nothing of a refund; a buyer's contest says what was bought was not delivered after payment (G11.8)
- **C-207** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Publications, splits and time (delivery)): "What is sold decides the class: a key delivered in keeping a vow is evidence beside the buyer's confirmation or contest, never replacing…" — unclear: d25 has a buyer's contest (G11.8) and delivery as a module (section 5.3); "confirmation" by a buyer is not said (the receiving side confirms, G11.7)
- **C-216** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Publications, splits and time (shares by reference)): "*The burden is passed to the step where the action truly takes place." — unclear: d25 places it in the buyer's assent and the payment naming the version (G11.7, G13.2)

- **C-223** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Publications, splits and time (splits)): "**Each participant checks**, once a purchase triggers the publication, whether its cut is at its flow address, or its vault where needed." — unclear: d25 has the receiving side's leg report confirming what it received (G11.5, K6); "triggers" is no longer a term (section 2)
- **C-260** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Keepers): "An act belonging to no agreement (a post, a bake, a rotation) has no keeper: only an anchor places it." — unclear: d25 places by the agreement's own place, or per anchoring where it names none (G7, G13.4)
- **C-262** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Keepers): "A keeper's later rotation never undoes what its records placed, shown as a dispute (Agreements rule 42)." — unclear: d25 needs from the Sign layer what it does when a key is replaced (section 8.1); place not defined
- **C-266** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Keepers): "A keeper's records hold in every copy of them, so a keeper that disappears loses nothing it had recorded." — unclear: d25 needs a place's answers to be signed (section 8.1); no grammar rule needs a server to remain (section 1)

- **C-425** (`02-MOR-MICS-v37.md`, Succession, grants and abandonment (grants)): "**Grants** delegate authority, with limits, revocably, and never beyond the power of whoever issued it: a collective's grant reaches onl…" — delegation: Terms in which a delegator gives an agent a power (G5.5); ended or narrowed by a new version; unclear whether d25 bounds it by the delegator's own power

## How this list was made

- The working list was built first, by Claude, from draft 24's "Words used in this MIR" against draft 25's section 2.
- Each file was read whole by a reader working from the working list and the sources above; Text and the README were read by one reader. The scribe then checked every quote against its file by script (each piece between "…" cuts is found word for word), checked that every line of the seven files naming Agreements, a publication, an ownership agreement, a keeper, a collective, a stake, a grant, a split service or a price tag is covered by a row, and added the eight places that check found missing (section headings of the MICS's Agreements part, and one README line). Two lines of Identity that say "agreements" in the plain sense ("followers and agreements move across", rule on succession, and its reasoning line) are not listed: they name no rule, field, type or concept of draft 24.
- Where a reader left out a sentence on purpose, it said so: in Money, sentences about request and push rails tied to no publication or purchase (those rails are Money's and Development's own); in Identity, uses of "disputed" that rest on Identity alone, and the `mwork` label; in Envelopes, lines that rest only on Money and the generic chain and fork wording of rules 14 and 15; in Text and the README, uses of "terms" that draft 25 reads alike.
- Some rows say "contrary in d25" where draft 25 reads the same case the other way. That is a description of the two texts side by side, not a reading chosen between them.
- The branch is `claude/old-agreements-pointers-core-lukvvs`: the session's harness requires that suffix on the branch the prompt names.

## The reader test

Before pushing, the file was given to a reader that had seen neither the ledger nor the prompt, asked to read "In plain words" and ten rows at random (C-047, C-133, C-248, C-377, C-451, C-560, C-689, C-742, C-888, C-1019). What it stumbled on, and what was done:

- Unexplained names (P1, the ledger, the MICS, the seven files, cMIP, tip, W, G, K, E, P, F): explained in "How to read this list".
- The counts seemed to add up twice (the three kinds make 1,034, and 202 "unclear" on top): "How to read this list" and plain words, line 4, now say that "unclear" sits inside the kinds. The plain words now name each kind.
- C-248 seemed to give the clock rule for a place: the cell is worded again. C-742's "unclear" did not say about what: worded again. C-560 cited the grant key for a scoped key: W50 now says that d24 calls the grant key Identity's scoped key. C-133 says "share" where W14 says "stake": W14 now names a holder's share. C-377: "nothing: dropped" is said to cover every item cited.
- **Not fixed:** C-1019 cites W14 though "stake" is not in its quote (the stake is the share the quote speaks of, "the rest hers"), and the finding numbers and step numbers inside quotes stay, since quotes are not changed. C-047 and others name draft 25 fields (`for`, `under`) that the working list does not list: the list is of what draft 25 dropped or renamed, not of all it has. The "what it leans on" column mixes W numbers, old rule numbers and words, and the rows are long: left as they are, since the prompt fixes the columns.
